            + ' title="' + esc(where.speaker) + '">'
            + '<span class="ex-t">' + esc(t) + '</span>'
            + '<span>' + where.html
            + ' <span class="tag' + (meaning ? ' is-meaning' : '') + '">' + esc(badgeFor(ev)) + '</span></span>'
            + '</div>';
        }).join('') + '</div>';
      } else if (call.brief) {
        summaryHtml = '<div class="clamp">' + hl(call.brief) + '</div>';
      } else {
        summaryHtml = '<div class="empty">No summary</div>';
      }

      const dt = fmtDate(call.datetime || call.call_date);
      const idShort = call.identity || '';

      div.innerHTML =
        relMark(call.relevance) +
        '<span class="date"><b>' + esc(dt.d) + '</b><span>' + esc(dt.t) + '</span></span>' +
        '<span class="dur">' + esc(call.duration_str || '—') + '</span>' +
        '<span class="party"><b>' + hl(call.outside || '—') + '</b>' +
          (idShort ? '<span title="' + esc(idShort) + '">' + hl(idShort) + '</span>' : '') +
        '</span>' +
        '<span class="sum">' + summaryHtml + '</span>' +
        '<span class="acts">' +
          (call.audio_filename ? '<a class="btn btn--primary" data-action="viewer" href="' + esc(Router.callHash(call)) + '">Viewer</a>' : '') +
          (call.pdf_filename ? '<a class="btn" data-action="pdf">PDF</a>' : '') +
        '</span>';
      return div;
    }

    function buildDetail(call, q) {
      const div = document.createElement('div');
      div.className = 'detail' + (call.relevance ? ' rel-' + call.relevance : '');
      div.dataset.idx = call.index;

      const briefHtml = call.brief
        ? '<h4>Summary</h4><p class="brief">' + hl(call.brief) + '</p>'
        : '<h4>Summary</h4><p class="brief" style="color:var(--ink-3)">No summary for this call.</p>';
      const idHtml = call.identity
        ? '<div class="id-block"><h4>Outside Party</h4><p>' + hl(call.identity) + '</p></div>'
        : '';

      let cuesHtml = '';
      const cues = call.cues || [];
      if (cues.length) {
        const rows = cues.map((cue, i) => {
          const noteBits = [];
          if (cue.note) noteBits.push('<b>' + hl(cue.note) + '</b>');
          if (cue.quote) noteBits.push('<span class="q">&ldquo;' + hl(cue.quote) + '&rdquo;</span>');
          const citeParts = [];
          if (cue.line_cite) citeParts.push('Tr. ' + esc(cue.line_cite));
          if (cue.page) citeParts.push('<a data-cue-pdf="' + i + '">PDF p.' + cue.page + '</a>');
          return '<tr data-cue-idx="' + i + '">'
            + '<td class="ts">' + esc(cue.ts) + '</td>'
            + '<td class="note">' + noteBits.join(' ') + '</td>'
            + '<td class="cite">' + citeParts.join(' · ') + '</td>'
            + '</tr>';
        }).join('');
        cuesHtml = '<div class="cues"><h4>Review Cues: ' + cues.length + '</h4><table>' + rows + '</table></div>';
      } else {
        cuesHtml = '<div class="cues"><h4>Review Cues</h4><p style="margin:0;font-size:13px;color:var(--ink-3)">None flagged for this call.</p></div>';
      }

      // Map cues to their nearest transcript turn for flag markers + jumping.
      const turns = Data.turnsOf(call);
      const cueTurnSet = new Set();
      cues.forEach(cue => {
        const ts = cue.t;
        if (ts == null) return;
        let best = -1;
        for (let i = 0; i < turns.length; i++) {
          if (turns[i].start <= ts + 0.25) best = i; else break;
        }
        if (best >= 0) cueTurnSet.add(best);
      });

      let turnsHtml;
      if (turns.length) {
        turnsHtml = turns.map((t, i) => {
          const html = hl(t.text);
          const isMatch = html.indexOf('<mark>') !== -1;
          const cls = 'turn' + (isMatch ? ' is-match' : '') + (cueTurnSet.has(i) ? ' has-cue' : '');
          return '<div class="' + cls + '" data-t="' + t.start + '" data-i="' + i + '">'
            + '<span class="who">' + esc(t.speaker) + '<span class="t">' + esc(secondsToLabel(t.start)) + '</span></span>'
            + '<span class="tx">' + html + '</span>'
            + '</div>';
        }).join('');
      } else {
        turnsHtml = '<div style="color:var(--ink-3);padding:14px 0">No transcript available.</div>';
      }

      const transHtml =
        '<div class="trans">'
        + '<div class="trans-head"><h4>Transcript</h4><span class="hint">Select any line to open the viewer at that moment</span>'
        + '<div class="trans-nav" id="transNav">'
        + '<span class="nav-label" id="navLabel"></span>'
        + '<button data-nav="-1" title="Previous">‹</button>'
        + '<button data-nav="1" title="Next">›</button>'
        + '</div></div>'
        + '<div class="trans-body" id="transBody">' + turnsHtml + '</div>'
        + '</div>';

      const metaCells = [];
      const push = (label, value) => { if (value) metaCells.push({ label, value }); };
      push('Call', String(call.index + 1).padStart(3, '0') + ' / ' + CALLS.length);
      push('Recorded', call.datetime);
      push('Number', call.outside);
      push('Facility', call.facility);
      push('Outcome', call.outcome);
      push('File', call.filename);
      const metaHtml = metaCells.map(m =>
        '<div class="m"><span class="lbl">' + esc(m.label) + '</span><span class="v">' + esc(m.value) + '</span></div>'
      ).join('');

      const cueFirst = cues.length ? cues[0].t : null;
      const actsHtml =
        '<div class="detail-acts">'
        + (call.pdf_filename ? '<a class="btn" data-action="pdf">Transcript PDF</a>' : '')
        + (call.audio_filename
            ? '<a class="btn btn--primary" data-action="viewer-cue" href="' + esc(Router.callHash(call, cueFirst)) + '">Open in Viewer'
              + (cueFirst != null ? ' <span class="mono-bit">' + esc(secondsToLabel(cueFirst)) + ' →</span>' : '')
              + '</a>'
            : '')
        + '</div>';

      div.innerHTML =
        '<div class="detail-grid">'
        + '<div>' + briefHtml + idHtml + '</div>'
        + cuesHtml
        + '</div>'
        + transHtml
        + '<div class="detail-meta">' + metaHtml + actsHtml + '</div>';
      return div;
    }

    // ── Transcript match / flag navigation ───────────────────────────────
    function initTransNav(q) {
      const body = document.getElementById('transBody');
      const navEl = document.getElementById('transNav');
      const label = document.getElementById('navLabel');
      if (!body || !navEl) return;

      const matches = Array.from(body.querySelectorAll('.turn.is-match'));
      const flags = Array.from(body.querySelectorAll('.turn.has-cue'));
      if (q && matches.length) {
        nav.targets = matches;
        label.className = 'nav-label';
        label.textContent = matches.length + ' MATCH' + (matches.length === 1 ? '' : 'ES');
      } else if (flags.length) {
        nav.targets = flags;
        label.className = 'nav-label';
        label.textContent = flags.length + ' FLAGGED';
      } else {
        nav.targets = [];
        navEl.classList.add('hidden');
        return;
      }
      navEl.classList.remove('hidden');
      nav.pos = -1;
      if (q && matches.length) jumpTrans(1);  // land on the first hit immediately
    }

    function jumpTrans(dir) {
      if (!nav.targets.length) return;
      const body = document.getElementById('transBody');
      if (!body) return;
      if (nav.pos >= 0 && nav.targets[nav.pos]) nav.targets[nav.pos].classList.remove('nav-current');
      nav.pos = ((nav.pos + dir) % nav.targets.length + nav.targets.length) % nav.targets.length;
      const el = nav.targets[nav.pos];
      el.classList.add('nav-current');
      body.scrollTop = el.offsetTop - body.clientHeight / 2 + el.clientHeight / 2;
      const label = document.getElementById('navLabel');
      if (label) {
        const base = label.textContent.replace(/^\d+ \/ \d+ · /, '');
        label.textContent = (nav.pos + 1) + ' / ' + nav.targets.length + ' · ' + base;
      }
    }

    function openCall(call, seconds) { Router.go(Router.callHash(call, seconds)); }

    // ── Events ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function bindEvents() {
      const rowsEl = document.getElementById('rows');
      rowsEl.addEventListener('click', e => {
        const navBtn = e.target.closest('[data-nav]');
        if (navBtn) { e.stopPropagation(); jumpTrans(parseInt(navBtn.dataset.nav, 10)); return; }

        const actionEl = e.target.closest('[data-action]');
        if (actionEl) {
          e.stopPropagation();
          const host = actionEl.closest('.row, .detail'); if (!host) return;
          const call = CALLS[parseInt(host.dataset.idx, 10)];
          if (!call) return;
          const action = actionEl.dataset.action;
          // The viewer buttons are real #call= links; the browser navigates.
          if (action === 'pdf') openPdf(call);
          return;
        }
        const excerpt = e.target.closest('.excerpt');
        if (excerpt) {
          e.stopPropagation();
          const row = excerpt.closest('.row'); if (!row) return;
          const call = CALLS[parseInt(row.dataset.idx, 10)];
          const t = excerpt.dataset.t;
          openCall(call, t ? parseFloat(t) : null);
          return;
        }
        const detail = e.target.closest('.detail');
        if (detail) {
          const call = CALLS[parseInt(detail.dataset.idx, 10)];
          if (!call) return;
          const cuePdfLink = e.target.closest('[data-cue-pdf]');
          if (cuePdfLink) {
            e.stopPropagation();
            const cue = call.cues[parseInt(cuePdfLink.dataset.cuePdf, 10)];
            if (cue && cue.page) openPdf(call, cue.page);
            return;
          }
          const cueRow = e.target.closest('[data-cue-idx]');
          if (cueRow) {
            e.stopPropagation();
            const cue = call.cues[parseInt(cueRow.dataset.cueIdx, 10)];
            if (cue) openCall(call, cue.t);
            return;
          }
          const turn = e.target.closest('.turn');
          if (turn) {
            e.stopPropagation();
            openCall(call, parseFloat(turn.dataset.t));
            return;
          }
          return;
        }
        const row = e.target.closest('.row');
        if (!row) return;
        const idx = parseInt(row.dataset.idx, 10);
        state.openIdx = state.openIdx === idx ? null : idx;
        renderTable();
      });

      let debounce = null;
      const searchInput = document.getElementById('searchInput');
      searchInput.addEventListener('input', e => {
        clearTimeout(debounce);
        debounce = setTimeout(() => {
          const was = state.query.trim();
          state.query = e.target.value; state.page = 1;
          if (state.query.trim() && !was) { state.sortKey = 'match'; state.sortDir = 'desc'; }  // a new query ranks by match
          renderTable();
        }, 180);
      });
      // A query the reviewer settles on (leaves the box, presses Enter) is
      // remembered for the recent-searches menu.
      searchInput.addEventListener('change', () => rememberSearch(searchInput.value));

      function setChipGroup(groupId, attr, value) {
        document.querySelectorAll('#' + groupId + ' .chip').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset[attr] === String(value)));
      }
      // A chip group whose data-<attr> value feeds `apply`, then re-renders.
      function bindChipGroup(groupId, attr, apply) {
        document.querySelectorAll('#' + groupId + ' .chip').forEach(btn => {
          btn.addEventListener('click', () => { apply(btn.dataset[attr]); setChipGroup(groupId, attr, btn.dataset[attr]); onFilterChange(); });
        });
      }
      bindChipGroup('searchMode', 'smart', v => { state.smart = v === '1'; });
      bindChipGroup('saidBy', 'spk', v => { state.speakers = parseInt(v, 10) || 0; });
      document.getElementById('outcomeFilter').addEventListener('change', e => { state.outcome = e.target.value; onFilterChange(); });
      document.getElementById('lengthFilter').addEventListener('change', e => { state.length = e.target.value; onFilterChange(); });

      function onFilterChange() { state.page = 1; renderTable(); }
      document.getElementById('dateFrom').addEventListener('change', e => { state.dateFrom = e.target.value; onFilterChange(); });
      document.getElementById('dateTo').addEventListener('change', e => { state.dateTo = e.target.value; onFilterChange(); });
      document.getElementById('phoneFilter').addEventListener('change', e => { state.phone = e.target.value; onFilterChange(); });

      function setRelevance(rel) {
        state.relevance = rel;
        document.querySelectorAll('.chip[data-rel]').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('active', b.dataset.rel === rel));
        onFilterChange();
      }
      // The mode switch is navigation: the hash names the mode, route() shows it.
      document.querySelectorAll('#modeGroup [data-mode]').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => Router.go(btn.dataset.mode === 'list' ? '#index' : '#' + btn.dataset.mode));
      });
      document.querySelectorAll('.chip[data-rel]').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => setRelevance(btn.dataset.rel));
      });
      document.querySelectorAll('.tally [data-tally]').forEach(el => {
        el.addEventListener('click', () => setRelevance(state.relevance === el.dataset.tally ? '' : el.dataset.tally));
      });
      document.getElementById('relAll').classList.add('active');

      document.getElementById('clearFilters').addEventListener('click', () => {
        state.query = ''; state.dateFrom = ''; state.dateTo = '';
        state.phone = ''; state.relevance = ''; state.outcome = ''; state.length = '';
        state.speakers = 0;
        if (state.sortKey === 'match') { state.sortKey = 'call_sort'; state.sortDir = 'asc'; }
        state.page = 1;
        document.getElementById('searchInput').value = '';
        document.getElementById('dateFrom').value = '';
        document.getElementById('dateTo').value = '';
        document.getElementById('phoneFilter').value = '';
        document.getElementById('outcomeFilter').value = '';
        document.getElementById('lengthFilter').value = '';
        setChipGroup('saidBy', 'spk', 0);
        document.querySelectorAll('.chip[data-rel]').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('active', b.id === 'relAll'));
        renderTable();
      });

      document.querySelectorAll('#cols [data-sort]').forEach(el => {
        el.addEventListener('click', () => {
          const key = el.dataset.sort;
          if (state.sortKey === key) {
            state.sortDir = state.sortDir === 'asc' ? 'desc' : 'asc';
          } else {
            state.sortKey = key;
            state.sortDir = (key === 'rel_rank' || key === 'duration') ? 'desc' : 'asc';
          }
          renderTable();
        });
      });

      document.getElementById('prevPage').addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (state.page > 1) { state.page--; renderTable(); window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' }); }
      });
      document.getElementById('nextPage').addEventListener('click', () => {
        state.page++; renderTable(); window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
      });

      window.addEventListener('resize', measureCtl);
      // The control bar's height can settle late while embedded fonts load.
      if (document.fonts && document.fonts.ready) document.fonts.ready.then(measureCtl);
    }

    const RECENT_KEY = 'jcs_recent_searches';
    function recentSearches() {
      try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(RECENT_KEY) || '[]') || []; } catch (e) { return []; }
    }
    function rememberSearch(q) {
      q = String(q || '').trim();
      if (q.length < 3) return;
      const list = [q].concat(recentSearches().filter(x => x.toLowerCase() !== q.toLowerCase())).slice(0, 8);
      try { localStorage.setItem(RECENT_KEY, JSON.stringify(list)); } catch (e) { /* storage unavailable */ }
      renderRecentSearches(list);
    }
    function renderRecentSearches(list) {
      const dl = document.getElementById('recentSearches');
      dl.innerHTML = (list || recentSearches()).map(q => '<option value="' + esc(q) + '"></option>').join('');
    }

    // Search loaded or changed state (the keyword index, then the meaning
    // layer, then each query vector): re-render an active query.
    function onSearchChange() {
      if (state.query.trim() || Search.state.lexical === 'missing') renderTable();
    }

    function init() {
      buildMasthead();
      populateSelect('phoneFilter', 'outside');
      populateSelect('outcomeFilter', 'outcome');
      renderRecentSearches();
      bindEvents();
      // The chart modes need dated calls; without any, the switch is not offered.
      document.getElementById('modeGroup').classList.toggle('hidden', !Data.dateExtent);
      renderTable();
    }

    // Filters, query, sort, the open row, and the mode all live in `state`,
    // which the call view never touches, so leaving and returning finds
    // them intact. `mode` comes from the hash; a chart mode falls back to
    // the list when no call has a date.
    function show(mode) {
      const next = (mode === 'calendar' || mode === 'timeline') && Data.dateExtent ? mode : 'list';
      const changed = next !== state.mode;
      state.mode = next;
      if (visible) { if (changed) renderTable(); return; }
      CallView.hide();
      document.title = CASE_NAME + ' — Call Index';
      root.classList.add('active-view');
      visible = true;
      if (changed) renderTable();
      measureCtl();
      window.scrollTo(0, savedScroll);
    }
    function hide() {
      if (!visible) return;
      savedScroll = window.scrollY || 0;
      root.classList.remove('active-view');
      visible = false;
    }

    return { init, show, hide, render: renderTable, state, isVisible: () => visible, onSearchChange };
  })();

  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // Call view
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  const CallView = (function () {
    const root = document.getElementById('call');
    let visible = false;

    // ── State ──
    let currentCallIdx = -1;
    let currentPage = 1;
    let totalPages = 1;
    let lineEntries = [];  // flat array of {page, line, text, start, end}
    const SPEEDS = [0.75, 1, 1.25, 1.5, 2];
    let speedIdx = 1;

    const playIcon = document.getElementById('playIcon');
    const timeCurrent = document.getElementById('timeCurrent');
    const timeDuration = document.getElementById('timeDuration');
    const progressFill = document.getElementById('progressFill');
    const progressInput = document.getElementById('progressInput');
    const playheadEl = document.getElementById('playhead');
    const cueTicksEl = document.getElementById('cueTicks');
    const volSlider = document.getElementById('volSlider');
    const speedBtn = document.getElementById('speedBtn');
    const presentBtn = document.getElementById('presentBtn');
    const searchInput = document.getElementById('transSearch');
    const searchClear = document.getElementById('transSearchClear');
    const transScroll = document.getElementById('transScroll');
    const summaryBody = document.getElementById('summaryBody');
    const summaryPane = document.getElementById('summaryPane');
    const summaryHeader = document.getElementById('summaryHeader');
    const summaryCollapseBtn = document.getElementById('summaryCollapseBtn');
    const sideStrip = document.getElementById('sideStrip');
    const railCalls = document.getElementById('railCalls');
    const callsToggle = document.getElementById('callsToggle');
    const callsStrip = document.getElementById('callsStrip');
    const prevBtn = document.getElementById('prevBtn');
    const nextBtn = document.getElementById('nextBtn');
    const pageInfo = document.getElementById('transPageInfo');
    const followState = document.getElementById('followState');
    const followMeta = document.getElementById('followMeta');
    const followResumeBtn = document.getElementById('followResumeBtn');
    const callPos = document.getElementById('callPos');
    const mainTitle = document.getElementById('mainTitle');
    const mainTitleFilename = document.getElementById('mainTitleFilename');
    const mainMeta = document.getElementById('mainMeta');
    const callsList = document.getElementById('callsList');
    const callCount = document.getElementById('callCount');
    const loadingOverlay = document.getElementById('loadingOverlay');
    let isScrubbing = false;
    let followPlayback = true;
    let activePlaybackEntryKey = '';
    let activePlaybackPage = 1;
    let activePlaybackTime = 0;
    let transcriptScrollGuard = 0;
    let pendingSeek = 0;
    // The line a whole-second deep link revealed, held as the active line
    // while the player sits paused at that time (the seek's own timeupdate
    // would otherwise re-mark the line still playing at t or nothing).
    let revealHold = null; // { start: "<line data-start>", at: seconds }

    function entryKey(entry) {
      if (!entry) return '';
      return String(entry.id || ((entry.page || 1) + ':' + (entry.line || 0) + ':' + (parseFloat(entry.start) || 0)));
    }

    function setTranscriptScrollGuard(ms) {
      clearTimeout(transcriptScrollGuard);
      transScroll.dataset.programmatic = '1';
      transcriptScrollGuard = setTimeout(() => {
        delete transScroll.dataset.programmatic;
      }, ms);
    }

    function updateFollowUi() {
      followState.classList.toggle('following', followPlayback);
      followState.textContent = followPlayback ? 'Following audio' : 'Browsing';
      if (lineEntries.length) {
        const bits = [formatTime(activePlaybackTime || 0)];
        if (activePlaybackPage) bits.push('p.' + activePlaybackPage);
        followMeta.textContent = bits.join(' · ');
      } else {
        followMeta.textContent = '';
      }
      followResumeBtn.hidden = followPlayback;
    }

    function pauseFollowPlayback() {
      if (!followPlayback) return;
      followPlayback = false;
      updateFollowUi();
    }

    function resumeFollowPlayback() {
      if (searchInput.value.trim()) {
        clearSearch();
      }
      followPlayback = true;
      activePlaybackEntryKey = '';
      updateFollowUi();
      syncActiveLines(ws.getCurrentTime(), { forceReveal: true });
    }

    // ── Call list rail ──
    function buildCallList() {
      callCount.textContent = CALLS.length + ' call' + (CALLS.length === 1 ? '' : 's');
      CALLS.forEach((call, i) => {
        const item = document.createElement('div');
        item.className = 'call-item';
        item.dataset.idx = i;
        item.title = call.filename;
        const dp = fmtDateParts(call.datetime);
        const when = dp ? dp.short + (dp.time ? ' · ' + dp.time : '') : '—';
        const cueBit = (call.cues && call.cues.length) ? ' · ' + call.cues.length + ' cue' + (call.cues.length === 1 ? '' : 's') : '';
        item.innerHTML =
          '<div class="l1"><span class="d">' + esc(when) + '</span><span>' + formatDur(call.duration) + '</span></div>' +
          '<div class="l2">' + esc(call.outside || call.filename) + '</div>' +
          '<div class="l3">' + relMark(call.relevance, cueBit) + '</div>';
        item.addEventListener('click', () => Router.go(Router.callHash(call)));
        callsList.appendChild(item);
      });
    }

    // Load one call into the view. `seekTo` (seconds) cues the audio and
    // reveals the line at that moment while staying paused; the transcript
    // is revealed right away and the audio seeks once its metadata loads.
    function loadCall(idx, seekSeconds) {
      currentCallIdx = idx;
      const call = CALLS[idx];

      // Update sidebar active state
      document.querySelectorAll('.call-item').forEach((el, i) => {
        el.classList.toggle('active', i === idx);
      });

      // Update header
      callPos.textContent = 'Call ' + String(idx + 1).padStart(3, '0') + ' of ' + CALLS.length;
      mainTitleFilename.textContent = call.filename;
      const dp = fmtDateParts(call.datetime);
      mainTitle.innerHTML = esc(call.outside || call.filename)
        + (dp ? '<span class="dash">·</span><span class="dt">' + esc(dp.long) + '</span>' : '');
      const metaParts = [dp && dp.time, formatDur(call.duration), call.inmate, call.facility, call.outcome].filter(Boolean);
      mainMeta.innerHTML = metaParts.map(esc).join('<span class="sep">·</span>');

      // Update audio
      loadingOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');
      ws.stop();
      resetProgressUI(call.duration || 0);
      pendingSeek = (seekSeconds != null && seekSeconds > 0) ? seekSeconds : 0;
      revealHold = null;
      loadAudioForCall(call);
      renderCueTicks(call);

      // Update analysis rail
      renderAnalysis(call);

      // Build line entries
      lineEntries = call.lines || [];
      totalPages = lineEntries.reduce((m, l) => Math.max(m, l.page || 1), 1);
      followPlayback = true;
      activePlaybackEntryKey = '';
      activePlaybackPage = 1;
      activePlaybackTime = 0;
      updateFollowUi();

      searchInput.value = '';
      searchClear.style.display = 'none';
      goToPage(1, { source: 'load' });

      if (pendingSeek > 0) {
        timeCurrent.textContent = formatTime(pendingSeek);
        syncProgressUI(pendingSeek, call.duration || 0);
        syncActiveLines(pendingSeek);
        syncActiveCue(pendingSeek);
        revealTranscriptAt(pendingSeek);
      }
    }

    // ── Analysis rail ──
    function renderAnalysis(call) {
      const parts = [];
      if (call.structured) {
        if (call.relevance) {
          parts.push('<div class="side-sec">' + relMark(call.relevance, ' relevance') + '</div>');
        }
        if (call.brief) {
          parts.push('<div class="side-sec"><h4>Summary</h4><p class="brief">' + esc(call.brief) + '</p></div>');
        }
        if (call.identity) {
          parts.push('<div class="side-sec"><h4>Outside Party</h4><p class="idp">' + esc(call.identity) + '</p></div>');
        }
        const cues = call.cues || [];
        if (cues.length) {
          const rows = cues.map((cue, i) =>
            '<div class="side-cue" data-cue-t="' + cue.t + '" data-cue-i="' + i + '">'
            + '<span class="t">' + esc(cue.ts) + '</span>'
            + '<span class="n">' + esc(cue.note || cue.quote || '') + '</span>'
            + '</div>'
          ).join('');
          parts.push('<div class="side-sec"><h4>Review Cues: ' + cues.length + '</h4>' + rows + '</div>');
        } else {
          parts.push('<div class="side-sec"><h4>Review Cues</h4><span class="side-empty">None flagged.</span></div>');
        }
      } else if (call.summary) {
        parts.push('<div class="side-sec"><h4>Summary</h4><p class="raw">' + esc(call.summary) + '</p></div>');
      } else {
        parts.push('<div class="side-sec"><span class="side-empty">No analysis available.</span></div>');
      }
      summaryBody.innerHTML = parts.join('');
      summaryBody.querySelectorAll('.side-cue').forEach(el => {
        el.addEventListener('click', () => seekTo(parseFloat(el.dataset.cueT) || 0));
      });
    }

    function renderCueTicks(call) {
      cueTicksEl.innerHTML = '';
      const dur = call.duration || 0;
      if (!dur) return;
      (call.cues || []).forEach(cue => {
        const tick = document.createElement('div');
        tick.className = 'cue-tick';
        tick.style.left = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, cue.t / dur * 100)) + '%';
        cueTicksEl.appendChild(tick);
      });
    }

    function syncActiveCue(t) {
      const call = CALLS[currentCallIdx];
      if (!call || !call.cues || !call.cues.length) return;
      let activeIdx = -1;
      for (let i = 0; i < call.cues.length; i++) {
        const start = call.cues[i].t;
        const end = (i + 1 < call.cues.length) ? call.cues[i + 1].t : start + 45;
        if (t >= start && t < Math.min(end, start + 45)) { activeIdx = i; break; }
      }
      summaryBody.querySelectorAll('.side-cue').forEach(el => {
        el.classList.toggle('now', parseInt(el.dataset.cueI, 10) === activeIdx);
      });
    }

    // ── Transcript rendering ──
    function renderPage(page, query) {
      const q = (query || '').trim();
      const mk = q ? Search.matcher(q) : null;
      const pages = {};
      lineEntries.forEach(l => {
        const p = l.page || 1;
        if (!pages[p]) pages[p] = [];
        pages[p].push(l);
      });

      transScroll.innerHTML = '';
      transScroll.classList.toggle('searching', !!q);
      const maxPage = Math.max(...Object.keys(pages).map(Number), 1);

      for (let p = 1; p <= maxPage; p++) {
        const pageEl = document.createElement('div');
        pageEl.className = 'trans-page' + (p === page ? ' active' : '');
        pageEl.dataset.page = p;
        let pageHasMatch = false;

        const lines = (pages[p] || []).sort((a, b) => (a.line || 0) - (b.line || 0));
        lines.forEach(entry => {
          const lineText = entry.rendered_text || entry.text || '';
          if (mk && mk.test(lineText)) pageHasMatch = true;
          const div = document.createElement('div');
          div.className = 'trans-line';
          div.dataset.start = entry.start || 0;
          div.dataset.end = entry.end || 0;

          const lineNum = '<span class="line-num">' + (entry.line || '') + '</span>';

          if (entry.words && entry.words.length > 0 && !q) {
            // Render prefix (speaker label / continuation indent) as plain text
            const rendered = entry.rendered_text || '';
            const plainText = entry.text || '';
            const prefixLen = rendered.length - plainText.length;
            const prefix = prefixLen > 0 ? esc(rendered.substring(0, prefixLen)) : '';

            const wordSpans = entry.words.map(w =>
              '<span class="word-ts" data-ws="' + w.s + '" data-we="' + w.e + '">' + esc(w.t) + '</span>'
            ).join(' ');
            div.innerHTML = lineNum + '<span class="line-text">' + prefix + wordSpans + '</span>';

            // Click on a word seeks to that word's timestamp
            div.querySelectorAll('.word-ts').forEach(span => {
              span.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
                e.stopPropagation();
                seekTo(parseFloat(span.dataset.ws) || 0);
              });
            });
            // Click on the line (but not a word) seeks to line start
            div.addEventListener('click', () => seekTo(parseFloat(entry.start) || 0));
          } else {
            // Fallback: no word data or search active — render as before
            div.addEventListener('click', () => seekTo(parseFloat(entry.start) || 0));
            const highlighted = mk ? mk.mark(lineText) : esc(lineText);
            div.innerHTML = lineNum + '<span class="line-text">' + highlighted + '</span>';
          }

          pageEl.appendChild(div);
        });

        const pageNoEl = document.createElement('div');
        pageNoEl.className = 'pageno';
        pageNoEl.textContent = p;
        pageEl.appendChild(pageNoEl);

        if (q && !pageHasMatch) pageEl.classList.add('no-match');
        transScroll.appendChild(pageEl);
      }

      updatePageNav(page, maxPage, q);
    }

    function goToPage(page, options) {
      const source = (options && options.source) || 'manual';
      currentPage = Math.max(1, Math.min(page, totalPages));
      renderPage(currentPage, searchInput.value);
      setTranscriptScrollGuard(source === 'playback' ? 700 : 250);
      transScroll.scrollTo({ top: 0 });
      if (source === 'manual') pauseFollowPlayback();
    }

    function goPage(delta) {
      goToPage(currentPage + delta, { source: 'manual' });
    }

    function updatePageNav(page, maxPage, query) {
      totalPages = maxPage;
      if (query) {
        const visiblePages = transScroll.querySelectorAll('.trans-page:not(.no-match)').length;
        pageInfo.textContent = visiblePages
          ? visiblePages + ' page' + (visiblePages === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' with matches'
          : 'No matches';
        prevBtn.disabled = true;
        nextBtn.disabled = true;
      } else {
        pageInfo.textContent = maxPage > 1 ? 'Page ' + page + ' / ' + maxPage : 'Page ' + page;
        prevBtn.disabled = page <= 1;
        nextBtn.disabled = page >= maxPage;
      }
    }

    function clearSearch() {
      searchInput.value = '';
      searchClear.style.display = 'none';
      renderPage(currentPage, '');
    }

    // ── Rail collapse (both sides) ──
    function toggleSummaryPane() {
      const collapsed = summaryPane.classList.toggle('collapsed');
      summaryCollapseBtn.innerHTML = collapsed ? '&#8249;' : '&#8250;';
      try { localStorage.setItem('jcs_summary_collapsed', collapsed ? '1' : '0'); } catch (e) {}
    }
    function toggleCallsRail() {
      const collapsed = railCalls.classList.toggle('collapsed');
      callsToggle.innerHTML = collapsed ? '&#8250;' : '&#8249;';
      try { localStorage.setItem('jcs_calls_collapsed', collapsed ? '1' : '0'); } catch (e) {}
    }

    // ── Present mode: strip the chrome, keep audio + printed transcript ──
    function togglePresent(force) {
      const on = typeof force === 'boolean' ? force : !document.body.classList.contains('present');
      document.body.classList.toggle('present', on);
      presentBtn.textContent = on ? 'Exit' : 'Present';
    }

    // ── Audio Setup ──
    const audioEl = document.getElementById('audio');
    const ws = {
      stop() {
        audioEl.pause();
        try { audioEl.currentTime = 0; } catch (e) {}
      },
      pause() {
        audioEl.pause();
      },
      load(url) {
        audioEl.src = url;
        audioEl.load();
      },
      getDuration() {
        return Number.isFinite(audioEl.duration) ? audioEl.duration : 0;
      },
      setTime(seconds) {
        try { audioEl.currentTime = seconds; } catch (e) {}
      },
      playPause() {
        if (audioEl.paused) {
          audioEl.play().catch(() => onPlayerError());
        } else {
          audioEl.pause();
        }
      },
      play() {
        return audioEl.play();
      },
      getCurrentTime() {
        return audioEl.currentTime || 0;
      },
      setPlaybackRate(rate) {
        audioEl.playbackRate = rate;
      },
      setVolume(volume) {
        audioEl.volume = volume;
      },
      isPlaying() {
        return !audioEl.paused;
      }
    };

    function onPlayerTimeUpdate(t) {
      timeCurrent.textContent = formatTime(t);
      if (!isScrubbing) syncProgressUI(t, ws.getDuration());
      syncActiveLines(t);
      syncActiveCue(t);
    }

    function onPlayerReady() {
      loadingOverlay.classList.add('hidden');
      const duration = ws.getDuration();
      timeDuration.textContent = formatTime(duration);
      syncProgressUI(ws.getCurrentTime(), duration);
      if (pendingSeek > 0) {
        // Deep-link seek: move the playhead AND the transcript view to the
        // target moment immediately, but stay paused until the user plays.
        const target = duration > 0 ? Math.max(0, Math.min(pendingSeek, duration)) : pendingSeek;
        ws.setTime(target);
        onPlayerTimeUpdate(target);
        revealTranscriptAt(target);
        pendingSeek = 0;
      }
    }

    // Jump the transcript view to the line for time t. A cue link carries the
    // cited line's start floored to a whole second (#call=…&t=MM:SS), and the
    // previous speaker's last word usually spans that second, so for a
    // whole-second t prefer the line that starts inside [t, t+1): that is the
    // cited line. Otherwise take the line containing t, then the next line.
    function revealTranscriptAt(t) {
      const wholeSecond = Number.isInteger(t);
      let startingWithin = null;
      let containing = null;
      let upcoming = null;
      for (const entry of lineEntries) {
        const start = parseFloat(entry.start) || 0;
        const end = parseFloat(entry.end) || 0;
        if (wholeSecond && start >= t && start < t + 1 && !startingWithin) startingWithin = entry;
        if (!containing && t >= start && t < (end || start + 5)) containing = entry;
        if (start >= t && (!upcoming || start < (parseFloat(upcoming.start) || 0))) upcoming = entry;
      }
      const entry = startingWithin || containing || upcoming;
      if (!entry) return;
      const page = entry.page || 1;
      if (page !== currentPage) goToPage(page, { source: 'playback' });
      const entryStart = String(entry.start || 0);
      const lineEl = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll('.trans-line'))
        .find(el => el.dataset.start === entryStart);
      if (lineEl) {
        setTranscriptScrollGuard(700);
        lineEl.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center' });
        // The time-based sync has already marked the line playing at t (the
        // previous speaker's last word for a cue link) or nothing at all:
        // move the highlight to the revealed line until playback takes over.
        if (startingWithin || !root.querySelector('.trans-line.active')) {
          root.querySelectorAll('.trans-line.active').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('active'));
          root.querySelectorAll('.word-ts.active-word').forEach(el => el.classList.remove('active-word'));
          lineEl.classList.add('active');
          revealHold = { start: entryStart, at: t };
        }
      }
    }

    function onPlayerError() {
      loadingOverlay.classList.add('hidden');
      showToast('Failed to load audio file', true);
    }

    function onPlayerPlay() {
      playIcon.innerHTML = '<path d="M6 19h4V5H6v14zm8-14v14h4V5h-4z"/>';
    }

    function onPlayerPause() {
      playIcon.innerHTML = '<path d="M8 5v14l11-7z"/>';
    }

    function loadAudioForCall(call) {
      const audioUrl = 'audio/' + encodeURIComponent(call.audio_filename || '');
      ws.load(audioUrl);
    }

    function togglePlay() {
      ws.playPause();
    }

    function seekTo(seconds) {
      revealHold = null;
      if (ws.getDuration() > 0) {
        const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(seconds, ws.getDuration()));
        ws.setTime(clamped);
        onPlayerTimeUpdate(clamped);
        if (!ws.isPlaying()) ws.play();
      } else {
        pendingSeek = seconds; // audio not loaded yet
      }
    }

    function skipBy(delta) {
      const t = ws.getCurrentTime() + delta;
      const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(t, ws.getDuration()));
      ws.setTime(clamped);
      onPlayerTimeUpdate(clamped);
    }

    function cycleSpeed() {
      speedIdx = (speedIdx + 1) % SPEEDS.length;
      const s = SPEEDS[speedIdx];
      ws.setPlaybackRate(s);
      speedBtn.textContent = s + '×';
      speedBtn.classList.toggle('modified', s !== 1);
      try { localStorage.setItem('jcs_playback_speed', String(s)); } catch (e) {}
    }

    function syncProgressUI(currentTime, duration) {
      const total = duration || 0;
      const safeTime = Math.max(0, currentTime || 0);
      const ratio = total > 0 ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, safeTime / total)) : 0;
      progressInput.value = String(Math.round(ratio * 1000));
      progressFill.style.width = (ratio * 100) + '%';
      playheadEl.style.left = (ratio * 100) + '%';
    }

    function resetProgressUI(duration) {
      timeCurrent.textContent = '0:00';
      timeDuration.textContent = formatTime(duration || 0);
      syncProgressUI(0, duration || 0);
    }

    function scrubToInputValue() {
      const duration = ws.getDuration();
      if (duration <= 0) return;
      const ratio = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (parseFloat(progressInput.value) || 0) / 1000));
      const seconds = ratio * duration;
      syncProgressUI(seconds, duration);
      ws.setTime(seconds);
    }

    function syncActiveLines(t, options) {
      const forceReveal = !!(options && options.forceReveal);
      // A revealed deep-link line stays active until playback starts or the
      // playhead leaves that second.
      if (revealHold && (ws.isPlaying() || Math.abs(t - revealHold.at) >= 1)) revealHold = null;
      const hold = revealHold;
      let activeEntry = null;
      for (const entry of lineEntries) {
        const start = parseFloat(entry.start) || 0;
        const end = parseFloat(entry.end) || 0;
        const hit = hold ? String(entry.start || 0) === hold.start : (t >= start && t < (end || start + 5));
        if (hit) {
          activeEntry = entry;
          break;
        }
      }

      const activePage = activeEntry ? (activeEntry.page || 1) : 0;
      const activeEntryKey = entryKey(activeEntry);
      activePlaybackTime = t;
      if (activePage) activePlaybackPage = activePage;
      updateFollowUi();

      const shouldFollow = followPlayback && !searchInput.value.trim();
      if (shouldFollow && activePage && activePage !== currentPage) {
        goToPage(activePage, { source: 'playback' });
      }

      let activeLineEl = null;
      root.querySelectorAll('.trans-line').forEach(el => {
        const start = parseFloat(el.dataset.start) || 0;
        const end = parseFloat(el.dataset.end) || 0;
        const isActive = hold ? el.dataset.start === hold.start : (t >= start && t < (end || start + 5));
        el.classList.toggle('active', isActive);
        if (isActive && !activeLineEl) activeLineEl = el;
        // Highlight active word within the line
        el.querySelectorAll('.word-ts').forEach(word => {
          const ws0 = parseFloat(word.dataset.ws) || 0;
          const we0 = parseFloat(word.dataset.we) || 0;
          word.classList.toggle('active-word', !hold && t >= ws0 && t < we0);
        });
      });

      if (shouldFollow && activeLineEl && (forceReveal || activeEntryKey !== activePlaybackEntryKey)) {
        setTranscriptScrollGuard(700);
        activeLineEl.scrollIntoView({ block: 'nearest', behavior: 'smooth' });
      }
      activePlaybackEntryKey = activeEntryKey;
    }

    // ── Keyboard shortcuts (call view only) ──
    function onKeydown(e) {
      if (!visible) return;
      if (e.target === searchInput) {
        if (e.key === 'Escape') { searchInput.blur(); }
        return;
      }
      if (e.key === ' ') { e.preventDefault(); togglePlay(); }
      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') skipBy(-5);
      if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') skipBy(5);
      if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') { e.preventDefault(); goPage(-1); }
      if (e.key === 'ArrowDown') { e.preventDefault(); goPage(1); }
      if (e.key === 'p' || e.key === 'P') togglePresent();
      if (e.key === 'Escape') togglePresent(false);
    }

    function bindEvents() {
      document.getElementById('playBtn').addEventListener('click', togglePlay);
      document.getElementById('skipBack').addEventListener('click', () => skipBy(-15));
      document.getElementById('skipFwd').addEventListener('click', () => skipBy(15));
      speedBtn.addEventListener('click', cycleSpeed);
      presentBtn.addEventListener('click', () => togglePresent());
      prevBtn.addEventListener('click', () => goPage(-1));
      nextBtn.addEventListener('click', () => goPage(1));
      searchClear.addEventListener('click', clearSearch);

      searchInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
        const q = searchInput.value.trim();
        searchClear.style.display = q ? 'block' : 'none';
        if (q) pauseFollowPlayback();
        renderPage(currentPage, q);
      });

      followResumeBtn.addEventListener('click', resumeFollowPlayback);
      transScroll.addEventListener('scroll', () => {
        if (!followPlayback) return;
        if (transScroll.dataset.programmatic === '1') return;
        pauseFollowPlayback();
      }, { passive: true });

      summaryCollapseBtn.addEventListener('click', (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); toggleSummaryPane(); });
      sideStrip.addEventListener('click', toggleSummaryPane);
      summaryHeader.addEventListener('click', () => {
        if (summaryPane.classList.contains('collapsed')) toggleSummaryPane();
      });
      try {
        if (localStorage.getItem('jcs_summary_collapsed') === '1') {
          summaryPane.classList.add('collapsed');
          summaryCollapseBtn.innerHTML = '&#8249;';
        }
      } catch (e) {}

      callsToggle.addEventListener('click', toggleCallsRail);
      callsStrip.addEventListener('click', toggleCallsRail);
      try {
        if (localStorage.getItem('jcs_calls_collapsed') === '1') {
          railCalls.classList.add('collapsed');
          callsToggle.innerHTML = '&#8250;';
        }
      } catch (e) {}

      audioEl.addEventListener('timeupdate', () => {
        // load() fires a timeupdate at 0 before metadata arrives; it must not
        // clobber a deep-link position that is waiting for the audio.
        if (pendingSeek > 0) return;
        onPlayerTimeUpdate(audioEl.currentTime || 0);
      });
      audioEl.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', onPlayerReady);
      audioEl.addEventListener('play', onPlayerPlay);
      audioEl.addEventListener('pause', onPlayerPause);
      audioEl.addEventListener('error', onPlayerError);
      audioEl.addEventListener('ended', () => {
        syncProgressUI(ws.getDuration(), ws.getDuration());
        onPlayerPause();
      });

      progressInput.addEventListener('pointerdown', () => {
        isScrubbing = true;
      });
      progressInput.addEventListener('pointerup', () => {
        isScrubbing = false;
        syncProgressUI(ws.getCurrentTime(), ws.getDuration());
      });
      progressInput.addEventListener('input', scrubToInputValue);
      progressInput.addEventListener('change', () => {
        scrubToInputValue();
        isScrubbing = false;
      });

      volSlider.addEventListener('input', () => {
        ws.setVolume(volSlider.value / 100);
      });
      ws.setVolume(0.8);

      try {
        const storedSpeed = parseFloat(localStorage.getItem('jcs_playback_speed') || '');
        const storedIdx = SPEEDS.indexOf(storedSpeed);
        if (storedIdx !== -1) speedIdx = storedIdx;
      } catch (e) {}
      ws.setPlaybackRate(SPEEDS[speedIdx]);
      speedBtn.textContent = SPEEDS[speedIdx] + '×';
      speedBtn.classList.toggle('modified', SPEEDS[speedIdx] !== 1);

      document.addEventListener('keydown', onKeydown);
    }

    function init() {
      buildCallList();
      bindEvents();
    }

    function show(idx, seekSeconds) {
      if (!visible) {
        IndexView.hide();
        document.body.classList.add('mode-call');
        root.classList.add('active-view');
        visible = true;
      }
      document.title = CASE_NAME + ' — Call Viewer';
      loadCall(idx, seekSeconds);
    }
    function hide() {
      if (!visible) return;
      togglePresent(false);
      ws.pause();
      document.body.classList.remove('mode-call');
      root.classList.remove('active-view');
      visible = false;
    }

    return {
      init, show, hide, togglePresent, seekTo, toggleCallsRail, toggleSummaryPane,
      isVisible: () => visible,
      currentIndex: () => currentCallIdx,
      currentPage: () => currentPage,
    };
  })();

  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  // Boot
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  Data.prepare();
  IndexView.init();
  CallView.init();
  Router.init();
  // The search sidecars load after first paint: keyword search first, then
  // the meaning layer when the package has one.
  Search.onChange(() => IndexView.onSearchChange());
  Search.init();

  // Exposed for the browser tests and console debugging; nothing in the
  // page calls through this object.
  window.JCS = {
    Data, IndexView, CallView, Router, Charts, Search,
    helpers: { esc, escRe, highlight, relMark, formatTime, formatDur, secondsToLabel, hashTime, parseTime, fmtDate, fmtDateParts },
  };
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
